2025-06-15T01:00:22.2254311Z *This section includes details on the pull request with code changes you have already submitted to fix the problem above.*
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2255329Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2255500Z <pr_title>
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2256169Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2256988Z </pr_title>
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2257210Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2257370Z <pr_description>
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2258871Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2259792Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2259895Z ## Problem
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2260262Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2260713Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2260994Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2261290Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2261581Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2261869Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2262108Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2262235Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2262729Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2263328Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2263413Z ## Solution
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2263526Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2263639Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2264124Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2264401Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2265133Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2266072Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2266794Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2267124Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2267232Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2267539Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2267739Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2267836Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2268069Z ```bash
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2268639Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2269281Z ```
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2269388Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2269490Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2269742Z ```bash
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2270470Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2271278Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2271498Z ```
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2271588Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2271736Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2271975Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2272072Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2272665Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2273184Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2273264Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2273517Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2274247Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2275010Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2275506Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2276046Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2276362Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2276744Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2277226Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2277307Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2277410Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2277414Z 
2025-06-15T01:00:22.2277500Z </pr_description>
